Every denial path performs the same key-derivation work, so response\n *      time does not tell an attacker whether an account exists, is locked, or\n *      whether the input was syntactically rejected.\n *   3. Usernames are canonicalised by removing only U+0020 and then refusing\n *      anything else <= U+0020.            return \"password is longer than \" + MAX_PASSWORD_CHARS + \" characters\";\n            }\n            if (pw[pw.length - 1] == '\u0000') {\n                return \"password ends in a NUL           throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"salt must be \" + SALT_BYTES + \" bytes\");\n            }\n            if (digest == null || digest.length < 16) {\n                throw new '\\r' || c == '\\n' || c == '\\t') {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (c < 0x21 || c == 0x7F) {\n                    sb.append('.');\n }\n            String t = line.trim();\n            if (t.isEmpty() || !t.startsWith(\"{\")) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            Map<String, String> fields = new               }\n                int keyEnd = t.indexOf('\"', keyStart + 1);\n                int colon = t.indexOf(':', keyEnd + 1);\n                if (keyEnd < 0 || colon < 0) {\n }\n            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(value.length());\n            for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n                char c = value.charAt(i);\n                if (c == '\\\\' && i + 5 < value.length() && value.charAt(i + 1) == 'u') {\n                    String hex = value.substring(i + 2, i + 6);\n                    try {\n                        sb.append((char)     }\n            return counts;\n        }\n\n        private static long lastSeqIn(Path file) {\n            long max = 0L;\n            try {\n                for (String line : Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                    AuditEvent event = parseLine(line);\n                    if (event != null && event.seq > max) {\n                        max = event.seq;\n                    }\n                }\n            } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n                return Long.MAX_VALUE / 2;\n            }\n            return max;\n        }\n\n        private static boolean hasHeader(Path file) {\n            try (BufferedReader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                String first = reader.readLine();\n                return first != null && first.startsWith(\"#\");\n            } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n\n        static long allocationFailures() {\n            return ALLOCATION_FAILURES.get();\n        }\n\n        static void rememberActor(String actor) {\n            if (SEEN_ACTORS.size() > 100_000) {\n                ALLOCATION_FAILURES.incrementAndGet();\n                return;\n            }\n            SEEN_ACTORS.add(actor);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------- accounts\n\n    static final class Account {\n        final String username;\n        final PasswordHash hash;\n        final Instant createdAt;\n        int failedAttempts;\n        Instant lockedUntil;\n        Instant lastLogin;\n\n        Account(String username, PasswordHash hash, Instant createdAt) {\n            this.username = username;\n            this.hash = hash;\n            this.createdAt = createdAt;\n        }\n\n        boolean lockedAt(Instant now) {\n            return lockedUntil != null && lockedUntil.isAfter(now);\n        }\n\n        boolean verify(char[] password) {\n            return hash.matchesDummyWork(password) || safeVerify(password);\n        }\n\n        private boolean safeVerify(char[] password) {\n            try {\n                byte[] probe = PasswordHash.derive(hash.algorithm, hash.iterations, hash.salt, password);\n                boolean ok = MessageDigest.isEqual(probe, hash.digest);\n                Arrays.fill(probe, (byte) 0);\n                return ok;\n            } catch (GeneralSecurityException | IllegalArgumentException e) {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** In-memory account store with the lockout policy. */\n    static final class Directory {\n        private final Map<String, Account> accounts = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n        private final SecureRandom random;\n        private final HashConfig config;\n        int maxFailures;\n        long lockMillis;\n        Supplier<Instant> now = Instant::now;\n\n        Directory(HashConfig config, SecureRandom random, int maxFailures, long lockMillis) {\n            this.config = config;\n            this.random = random;\n            this.maxFailures = maxFailures;\n            this.lockMillis = lockMillis;\n        }\n\n        synchronized Account get(String canonicalUsername) {\n            return accounts.get(canonicalUsername);\n        }\n\n        synchronized List<String> usernames() {\n            return new ArrayList<>(accounts.keySet());\n        }\n\n        synchronized int size() {\n            return accounts.size();\n        }\n\n        synchronized String create(String rawUsername, char[] password) {\n            String username = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(rawUsername);\n            if (username == null) {\n                return \"Username must be 3-64 characters using a-z, 0-9, '.', '_' or '-' (no other whitespace).\";\n            }\n            String problem = InputPolicy.passwordProblem(password);\n            if (problem != null) {\n                return \"Password refused: \" + problem + \".\";\n            }\n            if (accounts.containsKey(username)) {\n                return \"Account '\" + username + \"' already exists.\";\n            }\n            try {\n                byte[] salt = PasswordHash.randomSalt(random);\n                byte[] digest = PasswordHash.derive(config.algorithm, config.iterations, salt, password);\n                accounts.put(username, new Account(username,\n                        new PasswordHash(config.algorithm, config.iterations, salt, digest), now.get()));\n                Arrays.fill(digest, (byte) 0);\n                return null;\n            } catch (GeneralSecurityException e) {\n                return \"Could not create the account: \" + e.getMessage();\n            }\n        }\n\n        synchronized void put(Account account) {\n            accounts.put(account.username, account);\n        }\n\n        synchronized boolean unlock(String username) {\n            Account account = accounts.get(username);\n            if (account == null) {\n                return false;\n            }\n            account.lockedUntil = null;\n            account.failedAttempts = 0;\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        synchronized boolean locked(String username) {\n            Account account = accounts.get(username);\n            return account != null && account.lockedAt(now.get());\n        }\n\n        synchronized int failedAttempts(String username) {\n            Account account = accounts.get(username);\n            return account == null ? -1 : account.failedAttempts;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------ decisions\n\n    enum Outcome2 {\n        GRANTED, DENIED\n    }\n\n    /** What the caller may be told, plus the private reason that only the audit log sees. */\n    static final class Decision {\n        final Outcome outcome;\n        final String message;\n        final String reason;\n        final String actor;\n\n        Decision(Outcome outcome, String message, String reason, String actor) {\n            this.outcome = outcome;\n            this.message = message;\n            this.reason = reason;\n            this.actor = actor;\n        }\n\n        boolean granted() {\n            return outcome == Outcome.GRANTED;\n        }\n    }\n\n    static final class Session {\n        final String username;\n        final Instant startedAt;\n        final String token;\n\n        Session(String username, Instant startedAt, String token) {\n            this.username = username;\n            this.startedAt = startedAt;\n            this.token = token;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * The credential check. Control flow deliberately reaches the same\n     * key-derivation cost on every branch before deciding anything.\n     */\n    static final class Authenticator {\n        private static final String GENERIC_DENIAL = \"Access denied.\";\n        private static final String AUDIT_FAILURE_DENIAL =\n                \"Access denied: this attempt could not be recorded, so the portal will not continue.\";\n        private static final byte[] DUMMY_LABEL = \"admin-portal-dummy-salt-v1\".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n\n        private final Directory directory;\n        private final AuditLog audit;\n        private final SecureRandom random;\n        private final HashConfig config;\n\n        Authenticator(Directory directory, AuditLog audit, SecureRandom random, HashConfig config) {\n            this.directory = directory;\n            this.audit = audit;\n            this.random = random;\n            this.config = config;\n        }\n\n        Decision authenticate(String rawUsername, char[] password) {\n            String actor = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(rawUsername);\n            String passwordProblem = InputPolicy.passwordProblem(password);\n            if (actor == null || passwordProblem != null) {\n                String reason = actor == null ? \"malformed-username\" : \"rejected-password:\" + passwordProblem;\n                // Burn the same work as a real check before refusing.\n                PasswordHash.derive(config.algorithm, config.iterations,\n                        dummySalt(rawUsername == null ? \"\" : rawUsername), \"padding-placeholder\".toCharArray());\n                return record(Outcome.DENIED, \"\", reason, \"\", GENERIC_DENIAL);\n            }\n\n            Account account = directory.get(actor);\n            PasswordHash stored = account != null ? account.hash : dummyHash(actor);\n\n            boolean passwordOk = false;\n            byte[] probe = null;\n            try {\n                probe = PasswordHash.derive(stored.algorithm, stored.iterations, stored.salt, password);\n                passwordOk = MessageDigest.isEqual(probe, stored.digest);\n            } catch (GeneralSecurityException | IllegalArgumentException e) {\n                passwordOk = false;\n            } finally {\n                if (probe != null) {\n                    Arrays.fill(probe, (byte) 0);\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (account == null) {\n                return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"unknown-account\", \"\", GENERIC_DENIAL);\n            }\n\n            synchronized (directory) {\n                Instant now = directory.now.get();\n                if (!passwordOk) {\n                    account.failedAttempts++;\n                    if (account.failedAttempts >= directory.maxFailures) {\n                        account.lockedUntil = now.plusMillis(directory.lockMillis);\n                    }\n                    return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"invalid-credentials\",\n                            \"failed_attempts=\" + account.failedAttempts, GENERIC_DENIAL);\n                }\n                if (account.lockedAt(now)) {\n                    return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"locked\",\n                            \"locked_until=\" + account.lockedUntil, GENERIC_DENIAL);\n                }\n                account.failedAttempts = 0;\n                account.lockedUntil = null;\n                account.lastLogin = now;\n                return record(Outcome.GRANTED, actor, \"\", \"session_started\", GENERIC_DENIAL);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private Decision record(Outcome outcome, String actor, String reason, String detail, String message) {\n            AuditLog.rememberActor(actor);\n            try {\n                audit.append(outcome, AuditLog.sanitize(actor, 64), reason, AuditLog.sanitize(detail, 120));\n                return new Decision(outcome, message, reason, actor);\n            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException cannotRecord) {\n                try {\n                    audit.append(Outcome.ERROR, \"\", \"audit-write-failed\",\n                            AuditLog.sanitize(cannotRecord.getClass().getSimpleName(), 120));\n                } catch (IOException | RuntimeException alsoLost) {\n                    // Nothing left to try: the trail is unwritable.\n                }\n                return new Decision(Outcome.DENIED, AUDIT_FAILURE_DENIAL, \"audit-failure\", actor);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private PasswordHash dummyHash(String username) {\n            try {\n                byte[] digest = PasswordHash.derive(config.algorithm, config.iterations,\n                        dummySalt(username), unusedSecret());\n                PasswordHash hash = new PasswordHash(config.algorithm, config.iterations, dummySalt(username), digest);\n                Arrays.fill(digest, (byte) 0);\n                return hash;\n            } catch (GeneralSecurityException | IllegalArgumentException e) {\n                byte[] salt = PasswordHash.randomSalt(random);\n                byte[] digest = new byte[config.digestBytes];\n                random.nextBytes(digest);\n                return new PasswordHash(config.algorithm, config.iterations, salt, digest);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static byte[] dummySalt(String username) {\n            try {\n                MessageDigest sha = MessageDigest.getInstance(\"SHA-256\");\n                sha.update(DUMMY_LABEL);\n                sha.update((byte) ':');\n                sha.update(username.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n                byte[] full = sha.digest();\n                byte[] salt = new byte[PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES];\n                System.arraycopy(full, 0, salt, 0, PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES);\n                return salt;\n            } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"SHA-256 is required\", e);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static char[] unusedSecret() {\n            return \"unused-dummy-secret-0000\".toCharArray();\n        }\n\n        static String genericDenial() {\n            return GENERIC_DENIAL;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ----------------------------------------------------------------- portal\n\n    /** Holds the login state and refuses admin work without it. */\n    static final class Portal {\n        final Directory directory;\n        final AuditLog audit;\n        final Authenticator authenticator;\n        private final SecureRandom random;\n        private Session session;\n\n        Portal(Directory directory, AuditLog audit, SecureRandom random) {\n            this.directory = directory;\n            this.audit = audit;\n            this.random = random;\n            this.authenticator = new Authenticator(directory, audit, random, hashConfigOf(directory));\n        }\n\n        private static HashConfig hashConfigOf(Directory directory) {\n            return directory.config;\n        }\n\n        Decision login(String username, char[] password) {\n            Decision decision = authenticator.authenticate(username, password);\n            if (decision.granted()) {\n                byte[] token = new byte[12];\n                random.nextBytes(token);\n                session = new Session(decision.actor, Instant.now(),\n                        Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(token));\n            }\n            return decision;\n        }\n\n        synchronized void logout(String reason) {\n            if (session == null) {\n                return;\n            }\n            String who = session.username;\n            session = null;\n            try {\n                audit.append(Outcome.GRANTED, who, \"logout\", AuditLog.sanitize(reason, 120));\n            } catch (IOException ignored) {\n                // A failed logout record must not trap the operator in a session.\n            }\n        }\n\n        Session session() {\n            return session;\n        }\n\n        /** The single gate in front of admin-level functionality. */\n        String gate(java.util.function.Supplier<String> adminOnly) {\n            if (session == null) {\n                try {\n                    audit.append(Outcome.DENIED, \"\", \"admin-function-without-login\",\n                            AuditLog.sanitize(adminOnly.getClass().getSimpleName(), 120));\n                } catch (IOException ignored) {\n                    // Reporting the refusal matters more than this extra record.\n                }\n                return \"Not authenticated. Use 'login' first: admin functions require a session.\";\n            }\n            return adminOnly.get();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ----------------------------------------------------------- admin console\n\n    /** Admin-level functionality; every command goes through Portal.gate. */\n    static final class AdminConsole {\n        private final Portal portal;\n        private final boolean demoMode;\n\n        AdminConsole(Portal portal, boolean demoMode) {\n            this.portal = portal;\n            this.demoMode = demoMode;\n        }\n\n        String execute(String line) {\n            String trimmed = line.trim();\n            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {\n                return \"Type 'help' for the list of commands.\";\n            }\n            String[] parts = trimmed.split(\"\\\\s+\", 2);\n            String command = parts[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            String argument = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : \"\";\n            switch (command) {\n                case \"status\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        Session s = portal.session();\n                        return \"Session: \" + s.username + \" (token \" + s.token.substring(0, 6) + \"...)\"\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"Started: \" + s.startedAt + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"Audit log: \" + portal.audit.file();\n                    });\n                case \"accounts\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        if (portal.directory.size() == 0) {\n                            return \"No accounts.\";\n                        }\n                        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n                        for (String name : portal.directory.usernames()) {\n                            Account a = portal.directory.get(name);\n                            sb.append(name)\n                              .append(\"  created=\").append(a.createdAt.toString().substring(0, 10))\n                              .append(\"  failures=\").append(a.failedAttempts)\n                              .append(a.lockedAt(portal.directory.now.get()) ? \"  LOCKED\" : \"\")\n                              .append(a.lastLogin == null ? \"  never-logged-in\" : \"  last-login=\" + a.lastLogin)\n                              .append(System.lineSeparator());\n                        }\n                        return sb.toString().trim();\n                    });\n                case \"account\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        String name = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(argument);\n                        if (name == null) {\n                            return \"Usage: account <username>\";\n                        }\n                        Account a = portal.directory.get(name);\n                        if (a == null) {\n                            return \"No such account.\";\n                        }\n                        return \"username=\" + a.username + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"hash=\" + a.hash.serialize().replaceAll(\"[^:]+:(\\\\d+):.*\", \"PBKDF2 iterations $1\")\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"iterations=\" + a.hash.iterations\n                                + \"  salt_bytes=\" + a.hash.salt.length\n                                + \"  digest_bytes=\" + a.hash.digest.length + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"created=\" + a.createdAt + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"failed_attempts=\" + a.failedAttempts + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"locked_until=\" + (a.lockedUntil == null ? \"no\" : a.lockedUntil) + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"last_login=\" + (a.lastLogin == null ? \"never\" : a.lastLogin)\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"(the stored digest and salt are intentionally not printed)\";\n                    });\n                case \"audit-summary\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        try {\n                            SortedMap<String, Long> counts = portal.audit.counts();\n                            if (counts.isEmpty()) {\n                                return \"Audit log is empty.\";\n                            }\n                            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n                            for (Map.Entry<String, Long> e : counts.entrySet()) {\n                                sb.append(e.getValue()).append(\"  \").append(e.getKey()).append(System.lineSeparator());\n                            }\n                            return sb.toString().trim();\n                        } catch (IOException e) {\n                            return \"Could not read the audit log: \" + e.getMessage();\n                        }\n                    });\n                case \"recent\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        int n = 10;\n                        if (!argument.isEmpty()) {\n                            try {\n                                n = Math.min(200, Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(argument.trim())));\n                            } catch (NumberFormatException bad) {\n                                return \"Usage: recent [count]\";\n                            }\n                        }\n                        try {\n                            List<AuditEvent> events = portal.audit.review(n);\n                            if (events.isEmpty()) {\n                                return \"Audit log is empty.\";\n                            }\n                            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n                            for (AuditEvent e : events) {\n                                sb.append(e.toJson()).append(System.lineSeparator());\n                            }\n                            return sb.toString().trim();\n                        } catch (IOException e) {\n                            return \"Could not read the audit log: \" + e.getMessage();\n                        }\n                    });\n                case \"unlock\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        String name = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(argument);\n                        if (name == null) {\n                            return \"Usage: unlock <username>\";\n                        }\n                        return portal.directory.unlock(name)\n                                ? \"Unlocked '\" + name + \"' and reset its failure counter.\"\n                                : \"No such account.\";\n                    });\n                case \"create\":\n                    return portal.gate(() -> {\n                        if (!demoMode) {\n                            return \"Account creation is only enabled in --demo mode; put accounts in your own store.\";\n                        }\n                        String name = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(argument);\n                        if (name == null) {\n                            return \"Usage: create <username>\";\n                        }\n                        return null;\n                    });\n                case \"help\":\n                    return helpText();\n                default:\n                    return \"Unknown command '\" + command + \"'. Type 'help'.\";\n            }\n        }\n\n        static String helpText() {\n            return \"Commands (admin functions require a login):\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  login [username]   authenticate (prompts for the username, then the password)\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  whoami             show the current session\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  logout             drop the current session\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  status             session, log location\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  accounts           list accounts with lock and failure state\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  account <name>     one account, without revealing secret material\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  audit-summary      counts of every recorded outcome\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  recent [n]         last n audit records (default 10)\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  unlock <name>      clear a lockout and the failure counter\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  help               this list\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  quit / exit        leave the portal\";\n        }\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------- CLI\n\n    static final class Cli {\n        private final Portal portal;\n        private final AdminConsole console;\n        private final boolean allowEcho;\n        private final BufferedReader stdin;\n        private final java.io.Console consoleHandle;\n        private final PrintStream out;\n\n        Cli(Portal portal, AdminConsole console, BufferedReader stdin, PrintStream out) {\n            this.portal = portal;\n            this.console = console;\n            this.stdin = stdin;\n            this.out = out;\n            this.consoleHandle = System.console();\n            this.allowEcho = consoleHandle == null;\n        }\n\n        void run() throws IOException {\n            banner();\n            String line;\n            while (true) {\n                out.print(\"admin> \");\n                out.flush();\n                line = stdin.readLine();\n                if (line == null) {\n                    out.println();\n                    break;\n                }\n                String trimmed = line.trim();\n                if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                String command = trimmed.split(\"\\\\s+\", 2)[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n                if (command.equals(\"quit\") || command.equals(\"exit\")) {\n                    portal.logout(\"operator-quit\");\n                    out.println(\"Goodbye.\");\n                    break;\n                }\n                if (command.equals(\"logout\")) {\n                    portal.logout(\"operator-logout\");\n                    out.println(\"Logged out.\");\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (command.equals(\"login\")) {\n                    doLogin(trimmed);\n                    continue;\n                }\n                out.println(console.execute(trimmed));\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void doLogin(String trimmed) throws IOException {\n            String[] parts = trimmed.split(\"\\\\s+\", 2);\n            String username = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : prompt(\"Username: \");\n            if (username == null) {\n                out.println(\"Access denied: no username supplied.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            char[] password = readSecret(\"Password: \");\n            if (password == null) {\n                out.println(\"Access denied: no password supplied.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            try {\n                Decision decision = portal.login(username, password);\n                out.println(decision.message + (decision.granted() ? \" Welcome, \" + decision.actor + \".\" : \"\"));\n            } finally {\n                Arrays.fill(password, '\u0000');\n            }\n        }\n\n        private String prompt(String label) throws IOException {\n            out.print(label);\n            out.flush();\n            return stdin.readLine();\n        }\n\n        char[] readSecret(String label) throws IOException {\n            if (consoleHandle != null) {\n                char[] chars = consoleHandle.readPassword(label);\n                if (chars == null) {\n                    out.println();\n                }\n                return chars;\n            }\n            out.print(label + \" (input will be echoed - no terminal available)\");\n            out.flush();\n            String line = stdin.readLine();\n            if (line == null) {\n                out.println();\n                return null;\n            }\n            return line.toCharArray();\n        }\n\n        private void banner() {\n            out.println(\"Administrator portal. Type 'help' for commands, 'quit' to leave.\");\n            if (allowEcho) {\n                out.println(\"(Note: no controlling terminal, so password input is echoed.)\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------- main\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        PrintStream out = new PrintStream(new java.io.FileOutputStream(java.io.FileDescriptor.out, true), true, \"UTF-8\");\n        Path auditFile = Path.of(System.getProperty(\"portal.audit.log\", \"admin-portal-audit.jsonl\"));\n        boolean demo = false;\n        boolean review = false;\n        boolean summary = false;\n        int reviewCount = 20;\n\n        for (String arg : args) {\n            if (arg.startsWith(\"--audit-log=\")) {\n                auditFile = Path.of(arg.substring(\"--audit-log=\".length()));\n            } else if (arg.equals(\"--demo\")) {\n                demo = true;\n            } else if (arg.startsWith(\"--review\")) {\n                review = true;\n                int eq = arg.indexOf('=');\n                if (eq > 0) {\n                    reviewCount = Integer.parseInt(arg.substring(eq + 1));\n                }\n            } else if (arg.equals(\"--summary\")) {\n                summary = true;\n            } else if (arg.equals(\"--help\") || arg.equals(\"-h\")) {\n                out.println(usage());\n                return;\n            } else {\n                out.println(\"Unknown option: \" + arg);\n                out.println(usage());\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        AuditLog audit = new AuditLog(auditFile);\n\n        if (review || summary) {\n            printReview(out, audit, summary, reviewCount);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();\n        Directory directory = new Directory(HashConfig.production(), random, 5, 15 * 60_000L);\n        Portal portal = new Portal(directory, audit, random);\n\n        if (demo) {\n            seed(out, directory, \"admin\", \"Admin!Demo2026\");\n            seed(out, directory, \"ops\", \"Ops!Demo2026\");\n            out.println(\"Demo accounts loaded: admin / Admin!Demo2026 and ops / Ops!Demo2026\");\n        } else {\n            String envUser = System.getenv(\"PORTAL_ADMIN_USER\");\n            String envPassword = System.getenv(\"PORTAL_ADMIN_PASSWORD\");\n            char[] password = envPassword == null ? null : envPassword.toCharArray();\n            if (envUser != null && password != null) {\n                String error = directory.create(envUser, password);\n                if (error != null) {\n                    out.println(\"Bootstrap failed: \" + error);\n                } else {\n                    out.println(\"Bootstrap account ready: \" + envUser);\n                }\n            } else {\n                out.println(\"No accounts are configured.\");\n                out.println(\"Set PORTAL_ADMIN_USER and PORTAL_ADMIN_PASSWORD before starting,\");\n                out.println(\"or run with --demo to load two throwaway accounts.\");\n            }\n            if (password != null) {\n                Arrays.fill(password, '\u0000');\n            }\n        }\n\n        AdminConsole console = new AdminConsole(portal, demo);\n        BufferedReader stdin = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        new Cli(portal, console, stdin, out).run();\n    }\n\n    private static void seed(PrintStream out, Directory directory, String username, String password) {\n        String error = directory.create(username, password.toCharArray());\n        if (error != null) {\n            out.println(\"Seed failed for \" + username + \": \" + error);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void printReview(PrintStream out, AuditLog audit, boolean summary, int count) throws IOException {\n        out.println(\"# audit log: \" + audit.file().toAbsolutePath());\n        if (summary) {\n            SortedMap<String, Long> counts = audit.counts();\n            if (counts.isEmpty()) {\n                out.println(\"(no records)\");\n                return;\n            }\n            for (Map.Entry<String, Long> e : counts.entrySet()) {\n                out.printf(\"%6d  %s%n\", e.getValue(), e.getKey());\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        List<AuditEvent> events = audit.review(count);\n        if (events.isEmpty()) {\n            out.println(\"(no records)\");\n            return;\n        }\n        for (AuditEvent e : events) {\n            out.println(e.toJson());\n        }\n        out.println(\"# \" + events.size() + \" record(s) shown\");\n    }\n\n    private static String usage() {\n        return \"Usage: java -cp out portal.AdminPortal [options]\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                + \"  --audit-log=FILE   audit log location (default ./admin-portal-audit.jsonl,\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                + \"                     or -Dportal.audit.log=FILE)\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                + \"  --demo             load two throwaway accounts with printed passwords\" + System.lineSeparator()\n                + \" any other character &lt;= U+0020 (NUL, TAB,\n         * CR, LF, ...) makes the input invalid rather than being removed, so no\n         * invisible character can fold one identity onto another.\n         */\n        static String canonicalUsername(String raw) {\n            if (raw == null || raw.length() > MAX_USERNAME_CHARS) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            int from = 0;\n            int to = raw.length();\n            while (from < to && raw.charAt(from) == ' ') {\n                from++;\n            }\n            while (to > from && raw.charAt(to - 1) == ' ') {\n                to--;\n            }\n            if (from == to) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            for (int i = from; i < to; i++) {\n                if (raw.charAt(i) <= ' ') {\n                    return null;\n                }\n            }\n            String candidate = raw.substring(from, to).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            return LEGAL.matcher(candidate).matches() ? candidate : null;\n        }\n\n        /** Null means the password may be hashed. Refusal, never trimming. */\n        static String passwordProblem(char[] password) {\n            if (password == null) {\n                return \"no password supplied\";\n            }\n            if (password.length < MIN_PASSWORD_CHARS) {\n                return \"shorter than \" + MIN_PASSWORD_CHARS + \" characters\";\n            }\n            if (password.length > MAX_PASSWORD_CHARS) {\n                return \"longer than \" + MAX_PASSWORD_CHARS + \" characters\";\n            }\n            if (password[password.length - 1] == 0) {\n                return \"ends in a NUL         } else if (Character.isLowSurrogate(c)) {\n                    return \"contains an unpaired surrogate\";\n                }\n            }\n            return null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------ stored hashes\n\n    /** A PBKDF2 digest plus everything needed to re-derive and check it. */\n    static final class PasswordHash {\n        static final int SALT_BYTES = 16;\n        private static final int DIGEST_BITS = 256;\n\n        final String algorithm;\n        final int iterations;\n        final byte[] salt;\n        final byte[] digest;\n\n        PasswordHash(String algorithm, int iterations, byte[] salt, byte[] digest) {\n            if (algorithm == null || algorithm.isEmpty()) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"missing hash algorithm\");\n            }\n            if (iterations < 1000) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"iteration count too low: \" + }\n            if (digest == null || digest.length * 8 != DIGEST_BITS) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"digest must be \" + DIGEST_BITS + \" bits\");\n            }\n            this.algorithm = algorithm;\n            this.iterations = iterations;\n            this.salt = salt.clone();\n            this.digest = digest.clone();\n        }\n\n        static byte[] randomSalt(SecureRandom random) {\n            byte[] salt = new byte[SALT_BYTES];\n            random.nextBytes(salt);\n            return salt;\n        }\n\n        /** One PBKDF2 derivation. The caller zeroes the returned bytes. */\n        static byte[] derive(String algorithm, int iterations, byte[] salt, char[] password)\n                throws GeneralSecurityException {\n       }\n            PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(password, salt, iterations, DIGEST_BITS);\n            byte[] out;\n            try {\n                out = SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(algorithm).generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();\n            } finally {\n                spec.clearPassword();\n            }\n            if (out == null || out.length * 8 != DIGEST_BITS) {\n                throw new GeneralSecurityException(\"unexpected digest length from \" + algorithm);\n            }\n            return out;\n        }\n\n        String describe() {\n            return algorithm + \", \" + iterations + \" iterations, salt \" + salt.length\n                    + \" bytes, digest \" + digest.length + \" bytes\";\n        }\n\n        String serialize() {\n            Base64.Encoder encoder = Base64.getEncoder();\n            return algorithm + \":\" + iterations + \":\"\n                    + encoder.encodeToString(salt) + \":\" + encoder.encodeToString(digest);\n        }\n\n        static PasswordHash parse(String stored) {\n            if (stored == null) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"empty stored hash\");\n            }\n            String[] parts = stored.split(\":\", -1);\n            if (parts.length != 4) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"a stored hash has 4   }\n            return new PasswordHash(parts[0], iterations, decoder.decode(parts[2]), decoder.decode(parts[3]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // * Passwords never reach this class at all.\n     */\n    static final class AuditLog {\n        static final String HEADER = \"# admin-portal audit log v1 (append-only, one JSON record per        if (file.getParent() != null) {\n                Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());\n            }\n            boolean exists = Files.exists(file);\n            this.seq = exists ? lastSeqIn(file) : 0L;\n            this.headerWritten = exists && hasHeader(file);\n        }\n\n        Path file() {\n            return file;\n        }\n\n        synchronized long nextSeq() {\n            return seq;\n        }\n\n        synchronized void append(Outcome outcome, String actor, String reason, String detail) throws IOException {\n            long pending = seq + 1;\n            AuditEvent event = new AuditEvent(pending, Instant.now().toString(),\n                    sanitize(actor, 64), sanitize(reason, 96), sanitize(detail, 120));\n            List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>(2);\n            if (!headerWritten) {\n                lines.add(HEADER);\n                headerWritten = true;\n            }\n            lines.add(event.toJson());\n            try          headerWritten = existsHeaderOrFalse();\n                throw refused;\n            }\n            seq = pending;\n        }\n\n        private boolean existsHeaderOrFalse() {\n            return headerWritten;\n        }\n\n        /** Untrusted text: drop CR/LF/TAB, replace other control bytes, cap length. */\n        static String sanitize(String raw, int maxChars) {\n            if (raw == null) {\n                return \"\";\n            }\n            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(Math.min(raw.length(), maxChars));\n            for (int i = 0; i < raw.length() && sb.length() < maxChars; i++) {\n                char c = raw.charAt(i);\n                if (c == '\\r' || c == '\\n' || c == '\\t') {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (c < 0x21 || c == 0x7F) {\n                    sb.append('.');\n                } else if (c > 0x7E) {\n                    sb.append('?');\n                } else {\n                    sb.append(c);\n                }\n            }\n            return sb.toString();\n        }\n\n        static String jsonString(String value) {\n            String v = value == null ? \"\" : value;\n            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(v.length() + 8);\n            sb.append('\"');\n            for (int i = 0; i < v.length(); i++) {\n                char c = v.charAt(i);\n                if (c == '\"') {\n                    sb.append(\"\\\\\\\"\");\n                } else if (c == '\\\\') {\n                    sb.append(\"\\\\\\\\\");\n                } else if (c == '\\n' || c == '\\r' || c == '\\t') {\n                    // already removed by sanitize; drop defensively\n                } else if (c < 0x20 || c == 0x7F) {\n                    sb.append(String.format(\"\\\\u%04x\", (int) c));\n                } else {\n                    sb.append(c);\n                }\n            }\n            sb.append('\"');\n            return sb.toString();\n        }\n\n        /** {@code last} &lt;= 0 returns every record, otherwise the newest ones. */\n        List<AuditEvent> review(int last) throws IOException {\n            List<AuditEvent> all = new ArrayList<>();\n            if (!Files.exists(file)) {\n                return all;\n            }\n            for (String line : Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                AuditEvent event = parseLine(line);\n                if (event != null) {\n                    all.add(event);\n                }\n            }\n            if (last <= 0 || all.size() <= last) {\n                return all;\n            }\n            return new ArrayList<>(all.subList(all.size() - last, all.size()));\n        }\n\n        SortedMap<String, Long> counts() throws IOException {\n            SortedMap<String, Long> counts = new TreeMap<>();\n            for (AuditEvent event : review(0)) {\n                String key = event.outcome.key() + (event.reason.isEmpty() ? \"\" : \":\" + event.reason);\n                counts.merge(key, 1L, Long::sum);\n            }\n            return counts;\n        }\n\n        /** Returns null for the header and for anything unparseable. */\n        static AuditEvent parseLine(String line) {\n            if (line == null) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            String text = line.trim();\n            if (text.isEmpty() || !text.startsWith(\"{\")) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            Map<String, String> fields = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n            int cursor = 1;\n            while (cursor < text.length()) {\n                int keyStart = text.indexOf('\"', cursor);\n                if (keyStart < 0) {\n                    break;\n                }\n                int keyEnd = text.indexOf('\"', keyStart + 1);\n                int colon = keyEnd < 0 ? -1 : text.indexOf(':', keyEnd + 1);\n                if (colon < 0) {\n                    break;\n                }\n                String key = text.substring(keyStart + 1, keyEnd);\n                int valueStart = colon + 1;\n                while (valueStart < text.length() && text.charAt(valueStart) == ' ') {\n                    valueStart++;\n                }\n                if (valueStart >= text.length()) {\n                    break;\n                }\n                if (text.charAt(valueStart) == '\"') {\n                    int valueEnd = valueStart + 1;\n                    while (valueEnd < text.length() && !(text.charAt(valueEnd) == '\"' && text.charAt(valueEnd - 1) != '\\\\')) {\n                        valueEnd++;\n                    }\n                    if (valueEnd >= text.length()) {\n                        break;\n                    }\n                    fields.put(key, unescape(text.substring(valueStart + 1, valueEnd)));\n                    cursor = valueEnd + 1;\n                } else {\n                    int valueEnd = valueStart;\n                    while (valueEnd < text.length() && text.charAt(valueEnd) != ',' && text.charAt(valueEnd) != '}') {\n                        valueEnd++;\n                    }\n                    fields.put(key, text.substring(valueStart, valueEnd).trim());\n                    cursor = valueEnd;\n                }\n            }\n            String outcome = fields.get(\"outcome\");\n            if (outcome == null) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            Outcome parsed;\n            try {\n                parsed = Outcome.valueOf(outcome.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException unknownCode) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            long sequence;\n            try {\n                sequence = Long.parseLong(fields.getOrDefault(\"seq\", \"0\"));\n            } catch (NumberFormatException badNumber) {\n                sequence = -1L;\n            }\n            return new AuditEvent(sequence, fields.getOrDefault(\"occurred_at\", \"\"),\n                    fields.getOrDefault(\"actor\", \"\"), parsed,\n                    fields.getOrDefault(\"reason\", \"\"), fields.getOrDefault(\"detail\", \"\"));\n        }\n\n        private static String unescape(String value) {\n            if (value.indexOf('\\\\') < 0) {\n                return value;\n            }\n            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(value.length());\n            for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n                char c = value.charAt(i);\n                if (c == '\\\\' && i + 5 < value.length() && value.charAt(i + 1) == 'u') {\n                    try {\n                        sb.append((char) Integer.parseInt(value.substring(i + 2, i + 6), 16));\n                        i += 5;\n                        continue;\n                    } catch (NumberFormatException notHex) {\n                        // keep the backslash literally below\n                    }\n                }\n                if (c == '\\\\' && i + 1 < value.length()\n                        && (value.charAt(i + 1) == '\"' || value.charAt(i + 1) == '\\\\')) {\n                    sb.append(value.charAt(i + 1));\n                    i++;\n                    continue;\n                }\n                sb.append(c);\n            }\n            return sb.toString();\n        }\n\n        private static long lastSeqIn(Path file) throws IOException {\n            long max = 0L;\n            for (String line : Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                AuditEvent event = parseLine(line);\n                if (event != null && event.seq > max) {\n                    max = event.seq;\n                }\n            }\n            return max;\n        }\n\n        private static boolean hasHeader(Path file) throws IOException {\n            try (BufferedReader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                String first = reader.readLine();\n                return first != null && first.startsWith(\"#\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- accounts\n\n    static final class Account {\n        final String username;\n        final PasswordHash hash;\n        final Instant createdAt;\n        int failedAttempts;\n        Instant lockedUntil;\n        Instant lastLogin;\n\n        Account(String username, PasswordHash hash, Instant createdAt) {\n            this.username = username;\n            this.hash = hash;\n            this.createdAt = createdAt;\n        }\n\n        boolean lockedAt(Instant instant) {\n            return lockedUntil != null && lockedUntil.isAfter(instant);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** In-memory account store plus the lockout policy. */\n    static final class Directory {\n        private final Map<String, Account> accounts = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n        private final SecureRandom random;\n        final HashConfig config;\n        int maxFailures;\n        long lockMillis;\n        Supplier<Instant> now = Instant::now;\n\n        Directory(HashConfig config, SecureRandom random, int maxFailures, long lockMillis) {\n            this.config = config;\n            this.random = random;\n            this.maxFailures = maxFailures;\n            this.lockMillis = lockMillis;\n        }\n\n        synchronized Account get(String canonicalUsername) {\n            return accounts.get(canonicalUsername);\n        }\n\n        synchronized List<String> usernames() {\n            return new ArrayList<>(accounts.keySet());\n        }\n\n        synchronized int size() {\n            return accounts.size();\n        }\n\n        /** Returns null on success, otherwise the reason to show the operator. */\n        synchronized String create(String rawUsername, char[] password) {\n            String username = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(rawUsername);\n            if (username == null) {\n                return \"Username must be 3-64 characters of a-z, 0-9, '.', '_' or '-', with no other whitespace.\";\n            }\n            String problem = InputPolicy.passwordProblem(password);\n            if (problem != null) {\n                return \"Password refused: \" + problem + \".\";\n            }\n            if (accounts.containsKey(username)) {\n                return \"Account '\" + username + \"' already exists.\";\n            }\n            try {\n                byte[] salt = PasswordHash.randomSalt(random);\n                byte[] digest = PasswordHash.derive(config.algorithm, config.iterations, salt, password);\n                accounts.put(username, new Account(username,\n                        new PasswordHash(config.algorithm, config.iterations, salt, digest), now.get()));\n                Arrays.fill(digest, (char) 0 == 0 ? (byte) 0 : (byte) 0);\n                return null;\n            } catch (GeneralSecurityException failure) {\n                return \"Could not create the account: \" + failure.getMessage();\n            }\n        }\n\n        synchronized boolean unlock(String canonicalUsername) {\n            Account account = accounts.get(canonicalUsername);\n            if (account == null) {\n                return false;\n            }\n            account.lockedUntil = null;\n            account.failedAttempts = 0;\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        synchronized boolean locked(String canonicalUsername) {\n            Account account = accounts.get(canonicalUsername);\n            return account != null && account.lockedAt(now.get());\n        }\n\n        synchronized int failedAttempts(String canonicalUsername) {\n            Account account = accounts.get(canonicalUsername);\n            return account == null ? -1 : account.failedAttempts;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** What the caller may be told, plus the private reason only the audit log sees. */\n    static final class Decision {\n        final Outcome outcome;\n        final String message;\n        final String reason;\n        final String actor;\n\n        Decision(Outcome outcome, String message, String reason, String actor) {\n            this.outcome = outcome;\n            this.message = message;\n            this.reason = reason;\n            this.actor = actor;\n        }\n\n        boolean granted() {\n            return outcome == Outcome.GRANTED;\n        }\n    }\n\n    static final class Session {\n        final String username;\n        final Instant startedAt;\n        final String token;\n\n        Session(String username, Instant startedAt, String token) {\n            this.username = username;\n            this.startedAt = startedAt;\n            this.token = token;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * The credential check. Every path that could name an account pays exactly\n     * one key-derivation before anything is decided or reported.\n     */\n    static final class Authenticator {\n        private static final String GENERIC_DENIAL = \"Access denied.\";\n        private static final String GRANT_MESSAGE = \"Access granted.\";\n        private static final String AUDIT_FAILURE_DENIAL =\n                \"Access denied: this attempt could not be recorded, so the portal will not continue.\";\n        private static final byte[] DUMMY_LABEL = \"admin-portal-dummy-salt-v1:\".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n\n        private final Directory directory;\n        private final AuditLog audit;\n        private final SecureRandom random;\n\n        Authenticator(Directory directory, AuditLog audit, SecureRandom random) {\n            this.directory = directory;\n            this.audit = audit;\n            this.random = random;\n        }\n\n        Decision authenticate(String rawUsername, char[] password) {\n            String actor = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(rawUsername);\n            String passwordProblem = InputPolicy.passwordProblem(password);\n\n            // Cheap refusals: neither can name an account, so neither is allowed\n            // to reach the store (and neither pays for a derivation).\n            if (actor == null) {\n                return record(Outcome.DENIED, \"\", \"malformed-username\", \"\", GENERIC_DENIAL);\n            }\n            if (passwordProblem != null) {\n                return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"rejected-password\", passwordProblem, GENERIC_DENIAL);\n            }\n\n            Account account = directory.get(actor);\n            PasswordHash stored = account != null ? account.hash : dummyHash(actor);\n\n            boolean passwordOk = false;\n            byte[] probe = null;\n            try {\n                probe = PasswordHash.derive(stored.algorithm, stored.iterations, stored.salt, password);\n                passwordOk = MessageDigest.isEqual(probe, stored.digest);\n            } catch (GeneralSecurityException | IllegalArgumentException failure) {\n                passwordOk = false;\n            } finally {\n                if (probe != null) {\n                    Arrays.fill(probe, (byte) 0);\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (account == null) {\n                return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"unknown-account\", \"\", GENERIC_DENIAL);\n            }\n\n            synchronized (directory) {\n                Instant instant = directory.now.get();\n                if (!passwordOk) {\n                    account.failedAttempts++;\n                    if (account.failedAttempts >= directory.maxFailures) {\n                        account.lockedUntil = instant.plusMillis(directory.lockMillis);\n                    }\n                    return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"invalid-credentials\",\n                            \"failed_attempts=\" + account.failedAttempts, GENERIC_DENIAL);\n                }\n                if (account.lockedAt(instant)) {\n                    return record(Outcome.DENIED, actor, \"locked\",\n                            \"locked_until=\" + account.lockedUntil, GENERIC_DENIAL);\n                }\n                account.failedAttempts = 0;\n                account.lockedUntil = null;\n                account.lastLogin = instant;\n                return record(Outcome.GRANTED, actor, \"\", \"session_started\", GRANT_MESSAGE);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /** Recording is part of authenticating: no record means no access. */\n        private Decision record(Outcome outcome, String actor, String reason, String detail, String message) {\n            try {\n                audit.append(outcome, actor, reason, detail);\n                return new Decision(outcome, message, reason, actor);\n            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException cannotRecord) {\n                try {\n                    audit.append(Outcome.ERROR, actor, \"audit-write-failed\",\n                            cannotRecord.getClass().getSimpleName());\n                } catch (IOException | RuntimeException alsoLost) {\n                    // The trail is unwritable; there is nothing left to do but refuse.\n                }\n                return new Decision(Outcome.DENIED, AUDIT_FAILURE_DENIAL, \"audit-failure\", actor);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * A hash nobody can satisfy, built without deriving anything: the caller\n         * still runs one real derivation against it, which is the point.\n         */\n        private PasswordHash dummyHash(String username) {\n            byte[] salt = sha256(DUMMY_LABEL, username).length >= PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES\n                    ? Arrays.copyOf(sha256(DUMMY_LABEL, username), PasswordHash.SALT_BYTES)\n                    : PasswordHash.randomSalt(random);\n            byte[] digest = new byte[directory.config.digestBytes];\n            System.arraycopy(sha256(username.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), \"digest\"), 0, digest, 0,\n                    Math.min(digest.length, sha256(username.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), \"digest\").length));\n            if (digest.length != directory.config.digestBytes) {\n                random.nextBytes(digest);\n            }\n            return new PasswordHash(directory.config.algorithm, directory.config.iterations, salt, digest);\n        }\n\n        private static byte[] sha256(Object first, Object second) {\n            try {\n                MessageDigest sha = MessageDigest.getInstance(\"SHA-256\");\n                sha.update(String.valueOf(first).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n                sha.update(String.valueOf(second).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n                return sha.digest();\n            } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException impossible) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"SHA-256 is required by the portal\", impossible);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ portal\n\n    /** Holds the login state and refuses admin-level work without it. */\n    static final class Portal {\n        final Directory directory;\n        final AuditLog audit;\n        final Authenticator authenticator;\n        private final SecureRandom random;\n        private Session session;\n\n        Portal(Directory directory, AuditLog audit, SecureRandom random) {\n            this.directory = directory;\n            this.audit = audit;\n            this.random = random;\n            this.authenticator = new Authenticator(directory, audit, random);\n        }\n\n        Decision login(String username, char[] password) {\n            Decision decision = authenticator.authenticate(username, password);\n            if (decision.granted()) {\n                byte[] token = new byte[12];\n                random.nextBytes(token);\n                session = new Session(decision.actor, Instant.now(),\n                        Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(token));\n            }\n            return decision;\n        }\n\n        Session session() {\n            return session;\n        }\n\n        synchronized void logout(String reason) {\n            if (session == null) {\n                return;\n            }\n            String who = session.username;\n            session = null;\n            try {\n                audit.append(Outcome.GRANTED, who, \"logout\", reason);\n            } catch (IOException ignored) {\n                // A missing logout record must not trap the operator in a session.\n            }\n        }\n\n        String denyWithoutLogin(String command) {\n            try {\n                audit.append(Outcome.DENIED, \"\", \"admin-function-without-login\", command);\n            } catch (IOException ignored) {\n                // Reporting the refusal matters more than this one extra record.\n            }\n            return \"Not authenticated. Use 'login' first - admin functions require a session.\";\n        }\n\n        /** The single gate in front of admin-level functionality. */\n        String gate(String command, Supplier<String> adminOnly) {\n            if (session == null) {\n                return denyWithoutLogin(command);\n            }\n            return adminOnly.get();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ----------------------------------------------------------- admin console\n\n    /** Admin-level functionality; each command goes through Portal.gate. */\n    static final class AdminConsole {\n        private final Portal portal;\n\n        AdminConsole(Portal portal) {\n            this.portal = portal;\n        }\n\n        String execute(String line) {\n            String trimmed = line.trim();\n            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {\n                return \"Type 'help' for the list of commands.\";\n            }\n            String[] parts = trimmed.split(\"\\\\s+\", 2);\n            String command = parts[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            String argument = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].trim() : \"\";\n            switch (command) {\n                case \"status\":\n                    return portal.gate(command, () -> {\n                        Session s = portal.session();\n                        return \"user       \" + s.username\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"session    \" + s.token.substring(0, 6) + \"...\"\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"since      \" + s.startedAt\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"audit log  \" + portal.audit.file().toAbsolutePath();\n                    });\n                case \"accounts\":\n                    return portal.gate(command, () -> {\n                        List<String> names = portal.directory.usernames();\n                        if (names.isEmpty()) {\n                            return \"No accounts.\";\n                        }\n                        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n                        for (String name : names) {\n                            Account a = portal.directory.get(name);\n                            sb.append(String.format(\"%-16s created=%s failures=%d%s %s\",\n                                    name,\n                                    a.createdAt.toString().substring(0, 10),\n                                    a.failedAttempts,\n                                    a.lockedAt(portal.directory.now.get()) ? \" LOCKED     \" : \"            \",\n                                    a.lastLogin == null ? \"never logged in\" : \"last login \" + a.lastLogin))\n                              .append(System.lineSeparator());\n                        }\n                        return sb.toString().trim();\n                    });\n                case \"account\":\n                    return portal.gate(command, () -> {\n                        String name = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(argument);\n                        if (name == null) {\n                            return \"Usage: account <username>\";\n                        }\n                        Account a = portal.directory.get(name);\n                        if (a == null) {\n                            return \"No such account.\";\n                        }\n                        return \"username        \" + a.username\n                                + System.lineSeparator() + \"password storage \" + a.hash.describe()\n                                + System.lineSeparator() + \"created         \" + a.createdAt\n                                + System.lineSeparator() + \"failed attempts \" + a.failedAttempts\n                                + System.lineSeparator() + \"locked until    \"\n                                + (a.lockedUntil == null ? \"no\" : a.lockedUntil)\n                                + System.lineSeparator() + \"last login      \"\n                                + (a.lastLogin == null ? \"never\" : a.lastLogin)\n                                + System.lineSeparator()\n                                + \"(salt and digest are deliberately not displayed)\"\n                                + System.lineSeparator() + \"hash fingerprint  \" + fingerprint(a.hash);\n                    });\n                case \"audit-summary\":\n                    return portal.gate(command, () -> {\n                        try {\n                            SortedMap<String, Long> counts = portal.audit.counts();\n                            if (counts.isEmpty()) {\n                                return \"The audit log is empty.\";\n                            }\n                            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n                            for (Map.Entry<String, Long> entry : counts.entrySet()) {\n                                sb.append(String.format(\"%6d  %s\", entry.getValue(), entry.getKey()))\n                                  .append(System.lineSeparator());\n                            }\n                            return sb.toString().trim();\n                        } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n                            return \"Could not read the audit log: \" + unreadable.getMessage();\n                        }\n                    });\n                case \"recent\":\n                    return portal.gate(command, () -> {\n                        int n = 10;\n                        if (!argument.isEmpty()) {\n                            try {\n                                n = Math.min(500, Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(argument)));\n                            } catch (NumberFormatException badNumber) {\n                                return \"Usage: recent [count]\";\n                            }\n                        }\n                        try {\n                            List<AuditEvent> events = portal.audit.review(n);\n                            if (events.isEmpty()) {\n                                return \"The audit log is empty.\";\n                            }\n                            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n                            for (AuditEvent event : events) {\n                                sb.append(event.toJson()).append(System.lineSeparator());\n                            }\n                            return sb.toString().trim();\n                        } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n                            return \"Could not read the audit log: \" + unreadable.getMessage();\n                        }\n                    });\n                case \"unlock\":\n                    return portal.gate(command, () -> {\n                        String name = InputPolicy.canonicalUsername(argument);\n                        if (name == null) {\n                            return \"Usage: unlock <username>\";\n                        }\n                        try {\n                            portal.audit.append(Outcome.GRANTED, portal.session().username,\n                                    \"unlock-account\", name);\n                        } catch (IOException ignored) {\n                            // The unlock itself is what matters here.\n                        }\n                        return portal.directory.unlock(name)\n                                ? \"Unlocked '\" + name + \"' and reset its failure counter.\"\n                                : \"No such account.\";\n                    });\n                case \"help\":\n                    return helpText();\n                case \"login\":\n                case \"logout\":\n                case \"whoami\":\n                case \"quit\":\n                case \"exit\":\n                case \"create\":\n                    return \"'\" + command + \"' is handled by the console prompt, not here.\";\n                default:\n                    return \"Unknown command '\" + command + \"'. Type 'help'.\";\n            }\n        }\n\n        static String fingerprint(PasswordHash hash) {\n            byte[] first8 = Arrays.copyOf(hash.digest, 8);\n            return Base64.getEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(first8);\n        }\n\n        static String helpText() {\n            return \"Commands (everything except login/help/quit is admin-level and needs a session):\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  login [username]   authenticate (asks for the password, hidden where possible)\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  whoami             show the current session\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  logout             drop the current session\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  create <username>  provision a new admin account (asks for a password twice)\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  status             who you are and where the audit log lives\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  accounts           list accounts with lock and failure state\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  account <name>     one account, without revealing secret material\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  audit-summary      counts of every recorded outcome\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  recent [n]         the last n audit records (default 10)\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  unlock <name>      clear a lockout and reset the failure counter\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  help               this list\"\n                    + System.lineSeparator()\n                    + \"  quit / exit        leave the portal\";\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------- CLI\n\n    /** Interactive prompt. Reads the password without echoing when a tty exists. */\n    static final class Cli {\n        private final Portal portal;\n        private final AdminConsole console;\n        private final BufferedReader stdin;\n        private final java.io.Console tty;\n        private final PrintStream out;\n\n        Cli(Portal portal, AdminConsole console, BufferedReader stdin, PrintStream out) {\n            this.portal = portal;\n            this.console = console;\n            this.stdin = stdin;\n            this.out = out;\n            this.tty = System.console();\n        }\n\n        void run() throws IOException {\n            out.println(\"Administrator portal. Type 'help' for commands, 'quit' to leave.\");\n            if (tty == null) {\n                out.println(\"(No controlling terminal is available, so password input is echoed.)\");\n            }\n            while (true) {\n                out.print(\"admin> \");\n                out.flush();\n                String line = stdin.readLine();\n                if (line == null) {\n                    out.println();\n                    break;\n                }\n                String trimmed = line.trim();\n                if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                String[] parts = trimmed.split(\"\\\\s+\", 2);\n                String command = parts[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n                String argument = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].trim() : \"\";\n                switch (command) {\n                    case \"quit\":\n                    case \"exit\":\n                        portal.logout(\"operator-quit\");\n                        out.println(\"Goodbye.\");\n                        return;\n                    case \"logout\":\n                        portal.logout(\"operator-logout\");\n                        out.println(\"Logged out.\");\n                        continue;\n                    case \"login\":\n                        doLogin(argument);\n                        continue;\n                    case \"whoami\":\n                        out.println(portal.session() == null\n                                ? portal.denyWithoutLogin(\"whoami\")\n                                : \"You are '\" + portal.session().username + \"' since \" + portal.session().startedAt);\n                        continue;\n                    case \"create\":\n                        doCreate(argument);\n                        continue;\n                    default:\n                        out.println(console.execute(trimmed));\n                        continue;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void doLogin(String argument) throws IOException {\n            String username = argument.isEmpty() ? prompt(\"Username: \") : argument;\n            if (username == null) {\n                out.println(\"Access denied: no username supplied.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            char[] password = readSecret(\"Password: \");\n            if (password == null) {\n                out.println(\"Access denied: no password supplied.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            try {\n                Decision decision = portal.login(username, password);\n                out.println(decision.granted()\n                        ? decision.message + \" Welcome, \" + decision.actor + \".\"\n                        : decision.message);\n            } finally {\n                Arrays.fill(password, (byte) 0 == 0 ? Passwords are never stored or logged. They are verified against a\n *      PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 digest with a random per-account salt, compared in\n *      constant time.\n *   2. Every attempt that could name an account pays exactly one key derivation,\n *      whether the account exists, is locked, or the password is simply wrong,\n *      so response time does not disclose which of the three it was. Input that\n *      cannot name an account at all is refused cheaply: a fast refusal there\n *      leaks nothing and denies a cheap way to make the server do expensive work.\n *   3. Usernames are canonicalised by removing only U+0020 and then refusing\n *      anything else below U+0021. String.trim() also deletes control\n *      characters, which would fold \"admin\" plus a trailing tab onto the real\n *      account \"admin\".\n *   4. A password that ends in NUL or holds an unpaired surrogate is refused\n *      rather than hashed: PBEKeySpec converts char[] with its own lossy\n *      encoder, so two different secrets can produce the same digest.\n *   5. raw.length() && sb.length() < maxChars; i++) {\n                char c = raw.charAt(i);\n                if (c == '\\r' || c == '\\n' || c == '\\t') {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (c < 0x21 || c == 0x7F) {\n                    sb.append('.');\n                } else if (c > 0x7E) {\n                    sb.append('?');\n                } else {\n
